Master Data","","Berisi endpoint kategori product, status product, status pengerjaan, product,","dan layanan.","","\u003e **Catatan otorisasi (sesuai kode saat ini):**","\u003e - `kategory-product`, `status-product`, `status-pengerjaan`: **tanpa auth**.","\u003e - `products`: `GET` publik; `POST` butuh role `Admin`; `PATCH`/`DELETE` tanpa auth.","\u003e - `layanan`: butuh auth (`Admin`/`Petugas`/`Pelanggan`).","","---","","# Kategori Product","","Base path: `/api/v1/kategory-product`","","| Method | Endpoint | Body |","|---|---|---|","| GET | `/api/v1/kategory-product` | — |","| POST | `/api/v1/kategory-product` | `{ \"nama\": \"Percetakan\" }` |","| PATCH | `/api/v1/kategory-product/{id}` | `{ \"nama\": \"Percetakan Baru\" }` |","| DELETE | `/api/v1/kategory-product/{id}` | — |","","**GET Response 200**","","```json","[","  { \"id\": 2, \"nama\": \"Percetakan\" },","  { \"id\": 3, \"nama\": \"Packaging\" }","]","```","","**POST/PATCH/DELETE Response 200** — body kosong.","","---","","# Status Product","","Base path: `/api/v1/status-product`","","| Method | Endpoint | Body |","|---|---|---|","| GET | `/api/v1/status-product` | — |","| POST | `/api/v1/status-product` | `{ \"nama\": \"Tersedia\" }` |","| PATCH | `/api/v1/status-product/{id}` | `{ \"nama\": \"Habis\" }` |","| DELETE | `/api/v1/status-product/{id}` | — |","","**GET Response 200**","","```json","[","  { \"id\": 2, \"nama\": \"Tersedia\" },","  { \"id\": 3, \"nama\": \"Habis\" }","]","```","","---","","# Status Pengerjaan","","Base path: `/api/v1/status-pengerjaan`","","| Method | Endpoint | Body |","|---|---|---|","| GET | `/api/v1/status-pengerjaan` | — |","| POST | `/api/v1/status-pengerjaan` | `{ \"nama\": \"Sedang Berlangsung\" }` |","| PATCH | `/api/v1/status-pengerjaan/{id}` | `{ \"nama\": \"...\" }` |","| DELETE | `/api/v1/status-pengerjaan/{id}` | — |","","**GET Response 200**","","```json","[","  { \"id\": 1, \"nama\": \"Sedang Berlangsung\" },","  { \"id\": 2, \"nama\": \"Dibatalkan\" },","  { \"id\": 3, \"nama\": \"Selesai\" }","]","```","","\u003e Status di atas dipakai oleh `PUT /api/v1/pesanan/{id}/status` (Admin)","\u003e dan berubah otomatis saat pembayaran dibatalkan. Lihat","\u003e [pesanan.md](pesanan.md#put-apiv1pesananidstatus-admin).","","---","","# Product","","Base path: `/api/v1/products`","","| Method | Endpoint | Auth | Content-Type |","|---|---|---|---|","| GET | `/api/v1/products` | Tidak | — |","| GET | `/api/v1/products/{id}/ukuran` | Tidak | — |","| POST | `/api/v1/products` | `Admin` | `multipart/form-data` |","| PATCH | `/api/v1/products/{id}` | Tidak | `application/json` |","| DELETE | `/api/v1/products/{id}` | Tidak | — |","","## GET /api/v1/products","","**Response 200**","","```json","[","  {","    \"id\": 5,","    \"idKategoriProduct\": 2,","    \"idStatusProduct\": 2,","    \"nama\": \"Bodi Bakar\",","    \"deskripsi\": \"Cetak bodi motor\",","    \"imagePath\": \"/images/abc.png\",","    \"harga\": 100000.00,","    \"diskon\": 0,","    \"pricingMode\": \"Fixed\",","    \"dimensionUnit\": \"meter\",","    \"backgroundColor\": null,","    \"kategoryProduct\": { \"id\": 2, \"nama\": \"Percetakan\" },","    \"statusProduct\": { \"id\": 2, \"nama\": \"Tersedia\" }","  }","]","```","","## POST /api/v1/products","","**Form fields (multipart/form-data)**","","| Field | Tipe | Wajib |","|---|---|---|","| `IdKategoriProduct` | int | Ya |","| `IdStatusProduct` | int | Ya |","| `Nama` | string | Ya |","| `Deskripsi` | string | Tidak |","| `Harga` | decimal | Ya |","| `PricingMode` | string | Tidak | `Fixed` / `PerArea` / `PerLength` / `PerUnit`. Default `Fixed` |","| `DimensionUnit` | string | Tidak | `centimeter` / `meter`. Default `meter` |","| `BackgroundColor` | string | Tidak |","| `Image` | file | Tidak |","","\u003e Rincian arti `PricingMode`/`DimensionUnit`, rumus harga, dan cara","\u003e menambah product baru ada di [pricing-system.md](pricing-system.md).","","**Response 200** — mengembalikan payload product yang dikirim.","Catatan: `id` **belum terisi** pada response (server belum mengembalikan id).","","```js","const form = new FormData();","form.append(\"IdKategoriProduct\", 2);","form.append(\"IdStatusProduct\", 2);","form.append(\"Nama\", \"Bodi Bakar\");","form.append(\"Deskripsi\", \"Cetak bodi motor\");","form.append(\"Harga\", 100000);","form.append(\"Image\", fileInput.files[0]);","","await fetch(`${API}/api/v1/products`, {","  method: \"POST\",","  headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` }, // jangan set Content-Type manual","  body: form,","});","```","","## PATCH /api/v1/products/{id}","","**Request (JSON)**","","```json","{","  \"idKategoriProduct\": 2,","  \"idStatusProduct\": 2,","  \"nama\": \"Bodi Bakar\",","  \"deskripsi\": \"Cetak bodi motor\",","  \"imagePath\": \"/images/abc.png\",","  \"harga\": 120000,","  \"pricingMode\": \"PerArea\",","  \"dimensionUnit\": \"meter\",","  \"backgroundColor\": null","}","```","","**Response 200** — body kosong. **404** bila product tidak ditemukan.","","## GET /api/v1/products/{id}/ukuran","","Daftar varian (`Ukuran_Produk`) sebuah product: nama ukuran, harga, dan","opsi dimensi tetap (cm). Dipakai frontend untuk menampilkan pilihan ukuran","pada product ber-`pricingMode` `Fixed`.","","**Response 200**","","```json","[","  {","    \"id\": 3,","    \"idProduct\": 5,","    \"nama\": \"60 x 160 cm\",","    \"hargaTambahan\": 0.00,","    \"harga\": 150000.00,","    \"panjangCm\": 60,","    \"lebarCm\": 160","  }","]","```","","**Response 200** — `[]` bila product tidak punya varian.","","## DELETE /api/v1/products/{id}","","**Response 200** — body kosong. **404** bila tidak ditemukan.","","---","","# Layanan","","Base path: `/api/v1/layanan` — **butuh Bearer token**.","","| Method | Endpoint | Content-Type |","|---|---|---|","| GET | `/api/v1/layanan` | — |","| GET | `/api/v1/layanan/{id}` | — |","| POST | `/api/v1/layanan` | `multipart/form-data` |","| PATCH | `/api/v1/layanan/{id}` | `multipart/form-data` |","| DELETE | `/api/v1/layanan/{id}` | — |","","**Model**","","```json","{","  \"id\": 1,","  \"nama\": \"Cetak Spanduk\",","  \"deskripsi\": \"Spanduk berbagai ukuran\",","  \"imagePath\": \"/images/layanan/xxx.jpg\",","  \"backgroundColor\": \"#ffffff\"","}","```","","**Form fields POST/PATCH:** `Nama`, `Deskripsi`, `BackgroundColor`, `Image` (file).","","**Error:** `400` bila `Nama`/`Deskripsi` kosong, `404` bila data tidak ditemukan.","","```js","const form = new FormData();","form.append(\"Nama\", \"Cetak Spanduk\");","form.append(\"Deskripsi\", \"Spanduk berbagai ukuran\");","form.append(\"BackgroundColor\", \"#ffffff\");","form.append(\"Image\", fileInput.files[0]);","","await <p dir="auto">Berisi endpoint kategori product, status product, status pengerjaan, product,
dan layanan.</p>
<blockquote>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Catatan otorisasi (sesuai kode saat ini):</strong></p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><code>kategory-product</code>, <code>status-product</code>, <code>status-pengerjaan</code>: <strong>tanpa auth</strong>.</li>
<li><code>products</code>: <code>GET</code> publik; <code>POST</code> butuh role <code>Admin</code>; <code>PATCH</code>/<code>DELETE</code> tanpa auth.</li>
<li><code>layanan</code>: butuh auth (<code>Admin</code>/<code>Petugas</code>/<code>Pelanggan</code>).</li>
</ul>
</blockquote>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">POST /api/v1/products</h2><a id="user-content-post-apiv1products" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: POST /api/v1/products" href="#post-apiv1products"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Form fields (multipart/form-data)</strong></p>
<markdown-accessiblity-table><table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Field</th>
<th>Tipe</th>
<th>Wajib</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td><code>IdKategoriProduct</code></td>
<td>int</td>
<td>Ya</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>IdStatusProduct</code></td>
<td>int</td>
<td>Ya</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>Nama</code></td>
<td>string</td>
<td>Ya</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>Deskripsi</code></td>
<td>string</td>
<td>Tidak</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>Harga</code></td>
<td>decimal</td>
<td>Ya</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>PricingMode</code></td>
<td>string</td>
<td>Tidak</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>DimensionUnit</code></td>
<td>string</td>
<td>Tidak</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>BackgroundColor</code></td>
<td>string</td>
<td>Tidak</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>Image</code></td>
<td>file</td>
<td>Tidak</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></markdown-accessiblity-table>
<blockquote>
<p dir="auto">Rincian arti <code>PricingMode</code>/<code>DimensionUnit</code>, rumus harga, dan cara
menambah product baru ada di <a href="/MhdFarhannn/Katalog-Percetakan/blob/main/docs/pricing-system.md">pricing-system.md</a>.</p>
</blockquote>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Response 200</strong> — mengembalikan payload product yang dikirim.
Catatan: <code>id</code> <strong>belum terisi</strong> pada response (server belum mengembalikan id).</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-js notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="const form = new FormData();
form.append(&quot;IdKategoriProduct&quot;, 2);
form.append(&quot;IdStatusProduct&quot;, 2);
form.append(&quot;Nama&quot;, &quot;Bodi Bakar&quot;);
form.append(&quot;Deskripsi&quot;, &quot;Cetak bodi motor&quot;);
form.append(&quot;Harga&quot;, 100000);
form.append(&quot;Image&quot;, fileInput.files[0]);

await fetch(`${API}/api/v1/products`, {
  method: &quot;POST&quot;,
  headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` }, // jangan set Content-Type manual
  body: form,
});"><pre><span class="pl-k">const</span> <span class="pl-s1">form</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-k">new</span> <span class="pl-v">FormData</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
<span class="pl-s1">form</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">append</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s">"IdKategoriProduct"</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-c1">2</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
<span class="pl-s1">form</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">append</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s">"IdStatusProduct"</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-c1">2</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
<span class="pl-s1">form</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">append</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s">"Nama"</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-s">"Bodi Bakar"</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
<span class="pl-s1">form</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">append</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s">"Deskripsi"</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-s">"Cetak bodi motor"</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
<span class="pl-s1">form</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">append</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s">"Harga"</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-c1">100000</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
<span class="pl-s1">form</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">append</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s">"Image"</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-s1">fileInput</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-c1">files</span><span class="pl-kos">[</span><span class="pl-c1">0</span><span class="pl-kos">]</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>

<span class="pl-k">await</span> <span class="pl-en">fetch</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s">`<span class="pl-s1"><span class="pl-kos">${</span><span class="pl-c1">API</span><span class="pl-kos">}</span></span>/api/v1/products`</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
  <span class="pl-c1">method</span>: <span class="pl-s">"POST"</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
  <span class="pl-c1">headers</span>: <span class="pl-kos">{</span> <span class="pl-c1">Authorization</span>: <span class="pl-s">`Bearer <span class="pl-s1"><span class="pl-kos">${</span><span class="pl-s1">token</span><span class="pl-kos">}</span></span>`</span> <span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-c">// jangan set Content-Type manual</span>
  <span class="pl-c1">body</span>: <span class="pl-s1">form</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
<span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span></pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">PATCH /api/v1/products/{id}</h2><a id="user-content-patch-apiv1productsid" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: PATCH /api/v1/products/{id}" href="#patch-apiv1productsid"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Request (JSON)</strong></p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-json notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="{

  &quot;backgroundColor&quot;: null
}"><pre>{
  <span class="pl-ent">"idKategoriProduct"</span>: <span class="pl-c1">2</span>,

  <span class="pl-ent">"imagePath"</span>: <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>/images/abc.png<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>,
  <span class="pl-ent">"harga"</span>: <span class="pl-c1">120000</span>,
  <span class="pl-ent">"pricingMode"</span>: <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>PerArea<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>,
  <span class="pl-ent">"dimensionUnit"</span>: <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>meter<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>,

}</pre></div>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">GET /api/v1/products/{id}/ukuran</h2><a id="user-content-get-apiv1productsidukuran" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: GET /api/v1/products/{id}/ukuran" href="#get-apiv1productsidukuran"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Daftar varian (<code>Ukuran_Produk</code>) sebuah product: nama ukuran, harga, dan
opsi dimensi tetap (cm). Dipakai frontend untuk menampilkan pilihan ukuran
pada product ber-<code>pricingMode</code> <code>Fixed</code>.</p>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">DELETE /api/v1/products/{id}</h2><a id="user-content-delete-apiv1productsid" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: DELETE /api/v1/products/{id}" href="#delete-apiv1productsid"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h1 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Layanan</h1><a id="user-content-layanan" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Layanan" href="#layanan"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Base path: <code>/api/v1/layanan</code> — <strong>butuh Bearer token</strong>.</p>
<markdown-accessiblity-table><table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Method</th>
<th>Endpoint</th>
<th>Content-Type</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td>GET</td>
<td><code>/api/v1/layanan</code></td>
<td>—</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>GET</td>
<td><code>/api/v1/layanan/{id}</code></td>
<td>—</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>POST</td>
<td><code>/api/v1/layanan</code></td>
<td><code>multipart/form-data</code></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>PATCH</td>
<td><code>/api/v1/layanan/{id}</code></td>
<td><code>multipart/form-data</code></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>DELETE</td>
<td><code>/api/v1/layanan/{id}</code></td>
<td>—</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></markdown-accessiblity-table>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Model</strong></p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-json notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="{
  &quot;id&quot;: 1,
  &quot;nama&quot;: &quot;Cetak Spanduk&quot;,
  &quot;deskripsi&quot;: &quot;Spanduk berbagai ukuran&quot;,
  &quot;imagePath&quot;: &quot;/images/layanan/xxx.jpg&quot;,
  &quot;backgroundColor&quot;: &quot;#ffffff&quot;
}"><pre>{
  <span class="pl-ent">"id"</span>: <span class="pl-c1">1</span>,
  <span class="pl-ent">"nama"</span>: <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>Cetak Spanduk<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>,
  <span class="pl-ent">"deskripsi"</span>: <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>Spanduk berbagai ukuran<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>,
  <span class="pl-ent">"imagePath"</span>: <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>/images/layanan/xxx.jpg<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>,
  <span class="pl-ent">"backgroundColor"</span>: <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>#ffffff<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>
}</pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Form fields POST/PATCH:</strong> <code>Nama</code>, <code>Deskripsi</code>, <code>BackgroundColor</code>, <code>Image</code> (file).</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Error:</strong> <code>400</code> bila <code>Nama</code>/<code>Deskripsi</code> kosong, <code>404</code> bila data tidak ditemukan.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-js notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="const form = new FormData();
form.append(&quot;Nama&quot;, &quot;Cetak Spanduk&quot;);
form.append(&quot;Deskripsi&quot;, &quot;Spanduk berbagai ukuran&quot;);
form.append(&quot;BackgroundColor&quot;, &quot;#ffffff&quot;);
form.append(&quot;Image&quot;, fileInput.files[0]);

await fetch(`${API}/api/v1/layanan`, {
  method: &quot;POST&quot;,
  headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` },
  body: form,
});"><pre><span class="pl-k">const</span> <span class="pl-s1">form</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-k">new</span> <span class="pl-v">FormData</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
<span class="pl-s1">form</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">append</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s">"Nama"</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-s">"Cetak Spanduk"</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
<span class="pl-s1">form</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">append</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s">"Deskripsi"</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-s">"Spanduk berbagai ukuran"</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
<span class="pl-s1">form</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">append</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s">"BackgroundColor"</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-s">"#ffffff"</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
<span class="pl-s1">form</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">append</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s">"Image"</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-s1">fileInput</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-c1">files</span><span class="pl-kos">[</span><span class="pl-c1">0</span><span class="pl-kos">]</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>

<span class="pl-k">await</span> <span class="pl-en">fetch</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s">`<span class="pl-s1"><span class="pl-kos">${</span><span class="pl-c1">API</span><span class="pl-kos">}</span></span>/api/v1/layanan`</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
  <span class="pl-c1">method</span>: <span class="pl-s">"POST"</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
  <span class="pl-c1">headers</span>: <span class="pl-kos">{</span> <span class="pl-c1">Authorization</span>: <span class="pl-s">`Bearer <span class="pl-s1"><span class="pl-kos">${</span><span class="pl-s1">token</span><span class="pl-kos">}</span></span>`</span> <span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
  <span class="pl-c1">body</span>: <span class="pl-s1">form</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
<span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span></pre></div>
</article></div><div class="d-none"></div></section></div></div></div> </div> <!-- --> </div></div></div></div></div></div><div class="ScrollMarksContainer-module__scrollMarksContainer__Eu7uU" id="find-result-marks-container"></div><div class="d-none"></div><div class="d-none"></div></div> <!-- --> <!-- --> </div>
</react-app>

  <div id="ajax-error-message" class="ajax-error-message flash flash-error" hidden>
    <svg aria-hidden="true" data-component="Octicon" height="16" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" data-view-component="true" class="octicon octicon-alert">
    <path d="M6.457 1.047c.659-1.234 2.427-1.234 3.086 0l6.082 11.378A1.75 1.75 0 0 1 14.082 15H1.918a1.75 1.75 0 0 1-1.543-2.575Zm1.763.707a.25.25 0 0 0-.44 0L1.698 13.132a.25.25 0 0 0 .22.368h12.164a.25.25 0 0 0 .22-.368Zm.53 3.996v2.5a.75.75 0 0 1-1.5 0v-2.5a.75.75 0 0 1 1.5 0ZM9 11a1 1 0 1 1-2 0 1 1 0 0 1 2 0Z"></path>
</svg>

      <svg aria-hidden="true" data-component="Octicon" height="16" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" data-view-component="true" class="octicon octicon-x">
    <path d="M3.72 3.72a.75.75 0 0 1 1.06 0L8 6.94l3.22-3.22a.749.749 0 0 1 1.275.326.749.749 0 0 1-.215.734L9.06 8l3.22 3.22a.749.749 0 0 1-.326 1.275.749.749 0 0 1-.734-.215L8 9.06l-3.22 3.22a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042L6.94 8 3.72 4.78a.75.75 0 0 1 0-1.06Z"></path>
</svg>
    </button>
    You can’t perform that action at this time.
  </div>

    <template id="site-details-dialog">

    <summary role="button" aria-label="Close dialog"></summary>
    <details-dialog class="Box Box--overlay d-flex flex-column anim-fade-in fast hx_rsm-dialog hx_rsm-modal">
      <button class="Box-btn-octicon m-0 btn-octicon position-absolute right-0 top-0" type="button" aria-label="Close dialog" data-close-dialog>
        <svg aria-hidden="true" data-component="Octicon" height="16" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" data-view-component="true" class="octicon octicon-x">

      </button>
